# IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN

What gets built, in what order. Below the plan:

- **Build record:** what each round shipped.
- **Session notes:** dated, append-only.

BalayHub is an existing, deployed app, so "Sprint 0" (mockup) and "Sprint 1" (provisioning) from the Proverbs3 template are already behind us. The plan starts from where the app is today.

## Plan

### Now — before launch (founder items, see BLOCKERS.MD)

1. Rotate the exposed Gmail app password, and decide on the database password.
2. Backups and Hostinger snapshots, uptime monitor, PayMongo test booking, confirm prices.
3. Founder reviews the eight canon docs and corrects anything that's wrong.

### Next — Sprint A: SMS reminders to guests

**Goal:** guests get texts they actually read, for check-in, check-out, balance due and booking confirmation.

| # | Atom | Done looks like |
|---|---|---|
| A1 | SMS provider adapter (provider chosen in BLOCKERS), configured by env var and a test mode | Sending in tests writes to an outbox. A missing key turns SMS off with a clear message. |
| A2 | Workspace SMS settings: on/off per reminder type, English or Taglish, monthly limit | Owners and managers can change them. Staff can't. |
| A3 | Scheduler sends due reminders, at most once per stay per type, and only to guests with a mobile number | No duplicates after a restart. Cancelled stays are skipped. |
| A4 | SMS log on the reservation, plus a usage count against the monthly limit | Each text shows its time, type and status. Sending stops at the limit and warns the owner. |
| A5 | Phone number normalising (09xx → +639xx) and opt-out ("STOP") handling if the provider supports it | Invalid numbers are flagged, not sent. |
| A6 | Tests, a browser check, and doc updates (PRD, TRD, APP-FLOW, BACKEND-SCHEMA, DECISIONS) | `npm test` passes. The docs match the code. |

Before starting, run `/proverbs3-dfss` analysis on this feature so the requirements (CTQs) are written into DECISIONS.MD and the atoms above can be checked against them.

### Carry list for QA round 9 (from the round 8 re-verify, 2026-10-14)

- **Group rename writes one row per room.** Renaming a booking group should write one activity row for the group edit, not one per room (`stays.js`, audit inside the room loop).
- **Cleaning checklist label.** A checklist edit reads "Count: 9"; it should read "Changed: Checklist items" (`housekeeping.js` and the settings label).
- **One shared filter fix (third round of this class).** The one-owner page shows the filter bar when a link carries a dropped value; the address keeps the dropped value (`owners.js`, `cleanerpay.js`); and the report and list date-range boxes in `filters.js` have the same half-typed-year problem and still accept 1900 to 2200. Fix once in the shared filter bar: drop bad values before the first paint, keep the address in step, and apply the 2000 to 2100 range.
- **Calendar loads while typing.** Go-to-date loads the calendar for each complete date passed through while typing; add a short wait before loading.
- **Focus after a redraw.** Keyboard focus falls to the page after pricing save or remove, cleaner pay Mark paid, Undo and rate save, rent Record, refund Void, inbox Cancel and Retry, and the channels dialog.
- **Security carries.** Google sign-up one-time nonce instead of the 30-minute window; transaction plus `FOR UPDATE` on the unlocked compare-then-write saves (booking page settings, workspace edit, pricing rule, cleaning checklist, inbox template and automation, stays guest and group, maintenance schedule); router empty-parameter check on the last segment only.
- **Staff deposit response.** It carries internal ids (`organization_id`, `booking_id`, `recorded_by`), same as the owner; consider an allow-list.
- **Small items.** Admin support reply uses a toast on an empty send; `reservation.updated` keeps before and after amounts (documented exception in D-84).
- **Not exercised in round 8 (live stage):** forgot-password server-error focus, Google path, applied discount display, lease branch of the room-type pick, payout over-statement for an owner without units, cleaner Mark paid form date, glass, refund Void name, maintenance link, plan limit, automations tie-break (code-checked only).

### Small fixes found while writing these docs (do alongside Sprint A)

- **Starter plan:** show an "upgrade for AI drafts" hint. The server already says so, but the app ignores it.
- **Google sign-in:** check that a brand-new Google sign-up lands on workspace setup and not the landing page. Needs a browser check.
- **Launchpad footer:** point "contact support" to Help & support.
- **Trial banner:** show it only in the last few days, not the whole trial.
- **Dead code:** remove the unused `welcomeView`.
- **Old docs:** update `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, `docs/PROJECT-PLAN.md` ("Deferred" list) and `docs/SHORT-TERM-GUIDE.md` (the Launchpad replaced the Home checklist). Align README on 1–10 units and Node 22.
- **Design polish:** fix the dark-mode contrast and hard-coded colour list at the end of DESIGN-BRIEF.MD (soft-green text pairs, login caption, modal background, printing in dark mode, channel colour drift, small tap targets).

### Security and privacy fixes found while writing BACKEND-SCHEMA (do before or with Sprint A)

- ✅ 2026-10-13: Staff no longer receive the guest's private check-in link (it exposed guest phone and email). Covered by a test.
- ✅ 2026-10-13: Gate pass link hides guest ID photos when "Include guest ID photos" is off. The setting now covers both the email and the link, and applies to passes already sent.
- ✅ 2026-10-13: An hourly clean-up job deletes expired or idle sessions and old one-time sign-in tokens.
- ✅ 2026-10-13: The workspace export now includes support messages (without internal staff notes), ticket events and consent records.
- Open: when a stay moves to a unit in another property, its open cleaning task keeps its assignee, who may not be able to see the new property. The fix is to clear the assignee in that case.
- **Encryption key backups:** keep the encryption key out of the uploads backup by always setting DATA_ENCRYPTION_KEY and the VAPID keys in `deploy/.env`. The founder action is in BLOCKERS.
- **Lower priority:**
  - Store guest link tokens hashed.
  - Tighten the file path check.
  - Pass ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL through compose only if ever needed.

### Roadmap from the competitor research (2026-10-13)

Full reasoning is in `docs/research/00-SUMMARY.md`. Everything here is built inside BalayHub, with no integrations and no AI. Each phase still needs to be broken into atoms (and checked with `/proverbs3-dfss`) before it is built.

| Phase | What | Priority |
|---|---|---|
| 1. Money done right (✅ built 2026-10-13, except BIR invoices) | Itemised stay bill with extra charges and pay links; payment proof upload and bank transfer; cancellation policy presets and refund log; payment schedules and reminders; host net per booking; BIR-ready invoices and monthly tax summary (after a tax check) | Must |
| 2. Owners and co-hosts (✅ built 2026-10-13, spec: docs/specs/PHASE-2-OWNERS.md, decisions D-39 to D-45) | Owner portal login (statements, bookings, payouts, operations feed, own date blocks); fee models (gross %, net %, flat); expenses with receipts; review-then-send statements with PDF; payout record; repairs reserve; per-unit P&L; monthly emailed report | Must |
| 3. Operations (✅ built 2026-10-13, spec: docs/specs/PHASE-3-OPERATIONS.md, decisions D-48 to D-55) | Cleaner-only access (no money); cleaner calendar; status board with clean, dirty and inspected; inspections with scores; damage reports linked to deposits; cleaner pay and paid marker; supplies par levels; lost and found; work orders and recurring tasks for stays | Must / Should |
| 4. Guest experience and direct booking (✅ built 2026-10-13, spec: docs/specs/PHASE-4-GUESTS.md, decisions D-57 to D-64) | Guidebook (English and Taglish) and a timed Wi-Fi and door-code template; house rules acceptance; paid add-ons; quote-and-pay links and share links with source tags; private feedback; returning-guest codes; unpaid-booking follow-up; search-friendly listing pages | Should |
| 5. Pricing rules and team (✅ built 2026-10-13, spec: docs/specs/PHASE-5-PRICING-TEAM.md, decisions D-66 to D-74) | Gap nights, last-minute and far-out rules, events, PH holiday auto-fill, bulk rate and block changes, price preview; booking activity log, conversation assignment, @mentions, notification rules, unit tags; group bookings, room types, drag to another unit, daily close; sync-health alerts | Should / Could |

The order of SMS reminders (Sprint A, which needs an SMS provider) against Phase 1 is a founder decision (see BLOCKERS). The recommendation is Phase 1 first.

### Later

- **Sprint B: Facebook Messenger inbox.** Page messages appear in the Inbox next to email, with replies sent from BalayHub. Needs a Meta app and app review (BLOCKERS).
- **Candidate improvements**, not yet agreed:
  - booking page upgrades (gallery, map, reviews, share link)
  - smarter reports (month-to-month and year-to-year, best and worst listings)
  - recurring expenses
- **Quality passes:**
  - `/proverbs3-e2e` (journeys per role) and `/proverbs3-qa` (full click-through) on the live site, once a test workspace exists (BLOCKERS)

## Session notes

- **2026-10-13** · Competitor research done: 15 reports plus 2 follow-ups in `docs/research/`, summed up in `00-SUMMARY.md`. Integrations and AI features are excluded by founder request. The roadmap is Phases 1–5 above.

The focus-after-redraw class came back, but the guard itself worked: the sweep read focus 800 ms after the click, and the guard had a built-in 800 ms pause on the page body before it fell back to the heading. - **2026-10-15** · Design calls worth knowing when reading the code. **Check-in draft:** the server used to trust the guest's own count of ID photos. Rows from before stay finished. **Theme reversal:** D-88 exempted the theme buttons from 44px; a phone-size sweep and D-93's rule that every control is 44px settled it, so D-88, D-89 and D-92 carry an inline "superseded by D-95" note. **Supplies:** the founder's default was "refuse cleaners and change the matrix to No", but the cleaner job sheet's "Supplies used" picker reads that list. Refusing it would have broken the sheet, so the list route is closed to cleaners and a job-only route serves the sheet; the founder's intent (no wide list) holds. **Coupons:** BalayHub charges one prepaid period at a time, so a coupon covers billed months and "require card" means a one-time ₱20 card payment; auto-renew needs PayMongo Subscriptions approval (PayMongo research, 2026-10-15).
- **2026-10-15** · Security pass for round 15 (manual): PASS. Lows fixed: a payment against a released redemption no longer revives it; a checkout that fails (no PayMongo URL, or the free path) releases its slot at once; the preview checks `BILLING_OFF` before anything else; the docs now carry the coupon access rows. Info, carried: the admin coupon form should warn about unlimited free months.

### Carry list for QA round 14

*Verdict for round 13: not a clean round. The sweep found 55 findings (19 of the 24 significant ones were one focus bug, now fixed at the root); the re-verify held on the sidebar, focus, phone sizes, roles and the race test, and found small items, all below. Clean-round streak: 0/3.*

- **FOUNDER FEATURE REQUEST (2026-10-15), first item: a top bar like Guesty's** (D-90; screenshots in the round 14 carry notes). Overrides D-85's "Guesty features out of scope" for this one item. Adapt to BalayHub's brand and to small hosts; do not copy Guesty's look or names.
  1. **Global search**, centre of the top bar, "Type / to search". `/` focuses it from anywhere (not while typing in a field). Searches what the person may see (scoped by role on the server): reservations (guest name, confirmation code), guests, listings, properties, tenants, unit owners, quotes, maintenance requests, plus pages and settings by name, plus help articles. Grouped results, arrow keys and Enter, Escape closes, recent searches. One server endpoint (for example `GET /api/search?q=`) using existing access scoping, parameterised, limited results, rate-limited.
  2. **Bookmarks icon:** star the current page and list starred pages; saved per user on the server (a small table) so it follows them across devices.
  3. **"+" Add new menu:** Reservation, Listing, Property, Owner, Cleaning task, Expense, Invite teammate (and long-term: Tenant, Rent payment), filtered by role and workspace, each opening the existing `?new=1` dialog.
  4. **Notifications bell:** a panel with filters (Reservations, Payments, Cleaning, Messages, Owners), an unread count badge and mark as read, built on the existing events and activity log where possible; respects notification settings and role scope.
  5. **Help icon:** a panel with help-centre search, suggested articles for the current page and "Contact us" opening a support ticket (the existing help centre).
  6. **Profile avatar** (initials): My account, Settings, theme, Log out.
  7. **Phone:** the top bar keeps search (an icon opening full-screen search), "+" and the bell; bookmarks and help move into the profile menu or the More sheet. 44px targets, the D-89 focus rules, host app English.
  8. **Docs when built:** PRD F53 (planned entry exists), an APP-FLOW section, BACKEND-SCHEMA (the bookmarks table, the search endpoint, the notifications feed) and a DESIGN-BRIEF top bar section. D-90 already records the founder override.
- **SEV3, deposit Return dialog:** "The deduction can't be more than the deposit (₱X)." shows twice (the field error and the "Return to guest" line). `public/js/views/deposits.js` `returnLine()`: show the sentence once, at the field. Evidence `qa/r13v/R7.json`.
- **SEV3, Reports screen label:** the row reads "Whole-property expenses (dues etc.)"; the profit and loss screen and both CSVs read "Whole-property expenses". Drop the suffix and put "dues etc." in a hint (`public/js/views/stayreports.js` about line 231).
- **SEV3, page jumps after a save on a long list (founder-adjacent, smooth experience):** on Supplies, scrolled 600px down, Add stock then Save moves the page (`scrollY` 600 to 538) because the list is replaced and Chrome's scroll anchoring shifts it. Fix once in shared code (keep `window.scrollY` across in-place redraws in `ui.js`/`app.js`, or `overflow-anchor` rules), then check Supplies, Reservations, Housekeeping, Cleaner pay, Inbox and Owners lists. Evidence `qa/r13v/R1.json`.

- **Phone sizes still off the 44px rule (F02):** the public landing page's `.lp-top .btn--sm` (40px) and `.link-btn` (24 to 32px) on phones; the cleaner pay header "select all" checkbox is 20x20 (its label is 44px high; consider 24px).

- **Round 18 security pass, four Low items:**
  1. Purge locks the workspace row before `billing_payments`, while `markPaid` and `markPassed` lock the payment row first. A payment landing during a purge can deadlock; Postgres cancels one side and it retries. Fix: lock the workspace's `billing_payments` rows before the workspace in the purge.
  2. `subscriptions.startOne` re-checks only the subscription id under its lock. It should also require `auto_renew`, a saved card and no deletion scheduled.
  3. `attach()` holds a database connection across up to two PayMongo calls (20 seconds each); bounded by the rate limits.
  4. `filters.js` screen-size listener stays if the bar leaves before any resize (a small memory leak).
- **Founder question:** Supplies Property and Stock filters moved behind the Filters button on desktop too. Keep, or move back? (Also in BLOCKERS.)
- **Round 18 re-verify carries:** Profit and loss missing-fee note counts stays checking out today but its "See the bookings" list (Past stays) leaves them out (13 vs 12); rental Reports (#/reports) does not mark an empty custom-date box; double-clicking the confirm "Reopen day" leaves focus on BODY; the admin Delete workspace section has no gap or divider above it on phones; other long search hints may clip at 390px (properties, help, admin, owners, listings, reviews); a hidden "Pay ₱0.00 with Card ending undefined" button stays in the Plan & billing page after a card is removed.
- **Card attach follow-up (security Low/Info):** `markPassed` in client mode reads the workspace on a second pool connection (stall with 10 parallel attaches); the row lock is held across up to four PayMongo calls; the "attached" marker reuses `billing_payments.payment_method` (prefer an `attached_at` column via a new migration, or note it in BACKEND-SCHEMA); add tests for a failed 3-D Secure then a new card, and a succeeded 3-D Secure then a second attach.
- Not live-testable in the dev database: the Demo workspace delete refusal (no Demo workspace), PLATFORM_ADMIN_WORKSPACE (one platform admin), and the paid purge path (no card or subscription on the test workspace).
- The round 12 to 18 founder questions (BLOCKERS) and anything the round 18 re-verify carries.

### Carry list for QA round 28

0. **Top of round 28 (found at the round 27 gate, older than round 27):** on a phone (390 px), tapping a reservation card's guest name does not open the reservation; the page jumps to the end of the list. Cause as read: `labelTableWraps` (public/js/app.js) gives every `.table-wrap` tabindex 0, and on phones the reservations wrap is one box about 34,700 px tall, so a tap on card text focuses the box and the bottom-bar `focusin` handler scrolls by the box's whole distance below the bar. Repro: qa/r27v/a/. Also: the room price once read 2125.005000 when typed straight after picking a listing on New group booking (watch); the file kept on disk when an upload's save rolls back (security info); rename the old toast test title in qa-r15-s1; the statement Receipt link on screen and the Taglish quote email copy (not run at the gate); src/lib/paymongo.js's generic network sentence left as is (also used for refunds and payouts).
- **Re-check every round 27 fix by effect,** in the three browser groups of the round 27 re-verify (A: Back, scroll and router; B: forms, money rules and wording; C: card and billing). **Group A.1's Back restore is measured over at least 5 tries per page, with per-frame sampling of scrollY and the H1.**
- **Sweep scripts open a page by a link or a fresh load, never by `page.goto` of the address already on screen.** That is a same-address navigation, which used to wipe the page's key. It is fixed, but a script should not depend on it. **Click a control only where it can be seen:** before clicking, check that its centre is below the top bar (64 px at 1280) and above the bottom bar on phones. For scroll checks, prefer `page.mouse.click` at the measured centre or a keyboard press, because Playwright's retry scrolls the page itself.
- **Dev data:** restore the dev database before the re-verify (round 27 test data is in BLOCKERS round 27 (e)), with migration 040 applied, including the live ₱60,000 "QA r27 ops big cost" expense. Restart the server with the harness preload and `mark.sh unreachable off`.
